This is concerning.\n\nLet me think about what else could be wrong:\n\n1. **Action chunk usage**: The script uses REPLAN_STEPS=5, executing 5 actions from each predicted chunk of 50. But the Pi0.5 config has `n_action_steps: 50`, meaning it was trained to execute all 50 steps. Using only 5 might be suboptimal but shouldn't cause 0%.\n\n2. **Action interpretation**: The model predicts delta actions (position deltas + orientation deltas + gripper). \n\n3. **Gripper**: The 7th dimension is the gripper. In LIBERO, gripper is typically -1 (close) or +1 (open). The model's gripper output might be normalized differently.\n\n4. **State quaternion to axis-angle**: The conversion might be wrong. Let me check.\n\n5. **The model itself**: Maybe this finetuned model (pepijn223/pi05_libero_31_9_non_q) just has poor performance. The HF repo name contains \"non_q\" which might mean \"non-quantized\" or something else.\n\nNow I need to verify what action format LIBERO actually expects and whether I'm interpreting the model's outputs correctly.\n\nLooking at the normalization statistics, the xyz components are bounded in [-0.9375, 0.9375], which suggests delta positions in a normalized space. The gripper dimension has its own mean and standard deviation. I'm noticing the printed actions only show 4 dimensions, so I need to examine the full 7-dimensional action statistics to understand what's happening with the rotation and gripper components.\n\nLet me step back and try using LeRobot's built-in evaluation functionality instead of debugging this custom pipeline—it would leverage the standard preprocessor and postprocessor chains. The core issue is that LIBERO and robosuite expect 7-dimensional actions in the format [dx, dy, dz, dax, day, daz, gripper], where the gripper value should be in [-1, 1]. The model does output 7 dimensions, but I need to verify the unnormalization is working correctly for the rotation and gripper components. I'm realizing the standard LeRobot eval pipeline with `select_action` and the preprocessor is the right approach, but I'm hitting a blocker—the preprocessor needs the PaliGemma tokenizer which requires internet access I don't have. {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01Vn8sii3JZmZS6rQ1Znpz4i","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"thinking","thinking":"Key insight about the action normalization:\n\n- `action.mean[6]` = -0.0496 (gripper mean)\n- `action.std[6]` = 0.9988 (gripper std is ~1.0)\n\nSo the gripper dimension has mean close to 0 and std close to 1, meaning it's approximately in [-1, 1] range (open/close). The unnormalization `action * std + mean` for gripper would be approximately `action * 1.0 + (-0.05)` ≈ `action - 0.05`.\n\nIf the model predicts gripper values around 0 (neutral), the unnormalized value would be -0.05, which is close to -1 (close) in many LIBERO setups.\n \nI need to verify whether LIBERO's OffScreenRenderEnv expects discrete gripper signals at -1/+1 or if it handles continuous values, and I should also check what action format the previous SmolVLA evaluations were using. {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01GGtpveAeQup2hXHX4orpAx","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"thinking","thinking":"The config says `use_relative_actions: bool = False` by default, and the finetuned model doesn't override it (no \"relative\" in config.json). So actions are absolute, not relative/delta.\n\nBut wait, in LIBERO/robosuite, the controller typically expects **delta** actions (OSC_POSE controller uses delta position and delta rotation). If the model outputs absolute actions but the controller expects deltas, that would explain the 0% SR.\n\nActually, let me think about this more carefully. The Pi0.5 LIBERO fine-tuning was done on the LIBERO dataset. \n\nIn LeRobot's format, actions are likely stored as deltas already, so the model's predictions should be deltas too. When I unnormalize with `action * std + mean`, I'm converting from normalized space back to the original action space—delta position, delta rotation, and gripper values. Looking at the unnormalized actions from the dry-run, the values like [-0.010, -0.088, 0.027] and [-0.067, 0.052, -0.366] seem reasonable for small delta movements.\n\nNow I'm wondering if there's a mismatch between how the model's action chunk is structured and how the evaluation uses it. The model predicts 50 steps with `chunk_size=50` and `n_action_steps=50`, but the eval script only uses 5 steps (`REPLAN_STEPS=5`), meaning we're replanning every 5 steps instead of using the full predicted sequence. This could be a temporal resolution issue if the LIBERO dataset was collected at a different action frequency than what the eval expects. I need to check if there's an action representation mismatch between what the model outputs and what the environment expects. The LIBERO environment uses robosuite's OSC_POSE controller which takes a 7-dimensional action vector: [dx, dy, dz, dax, day, daz, gripper], where the gripper value ranges from -1 to 1. {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01NBepXLUh6t5zcYSZDZJqCt","type":"tool_result","content":"{\"result\":\"{\\\"success\\\": true, \\\"data\\\": {\\\"stdout\\\": \\\"import dataclasses\\\\n\\\\nimport einops\\\\nimport numpy as np\\\\n\\\\nfrom openpi import transforms\\\\nfrom openpi.models import model as _model\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef make_libero_example() -> dict:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Creates a random input example for the Libero policy.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    return {\\\\n        \\\\\\\"observation/state\\\\\\\": np.random.rand(8),\\\\n        \\\\\\\"observation/image\\\\\\\": np.random.randint(256, size=(224, 224, 3), dtype=np.uint8),\\\\n        \\\\\\\"observation/wrist_image\\\\\\\": np.random.randint(256, size=(224, 224, 3), dtype=np.uint8),\\\\n        \\\\\\\"prompt\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"do something\\\\\\\",\\\\n    }\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef _parse_image(image) -> np.ndarray:\\\\n    image = np.asarray(image)\\\\n    if np.issubdtype(image.dtype, np.floating):\\\\n        image = (255 * image).astype(np.uint8)\\\\n    if image.shape[0] == 3:\\\\n        image = einops.rearrange(image, \\\\\\\"c h w -> h w c\\\\\\\")\\\\n    return image\\\\n\\\\n\\\\n@dataclasses.dataclass(frozen=True)\\\\nclass LiberoInputs(transforms.DataTransformFn):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    This class is used to convert inputs to the model to the expected format. It is used for both training and inference.\\\\n\\\\n    For your own dataset, you can copy this class and modify the keys based on the comments below to pipe\\\\n    the correct elements of your dataset into the model.\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n    # Determines which model will be used.\\\\n    # Do not change this for your own dataset.\\\\n    model_type: _model.ModelType\\\\n\\\\n    def __call__(self, data: dict) -> dict:\\\\n        # Possibly need to parse images to uint8 (H,W,C) since LeRobot automatically\\\\n        # stores as float32 (C,H,W), gets skipped for policy inference.\\\\n        # Keep this for your own dataset, but if your dataset stores the images\\\\n        # in a different key than \\\\\\\"observation/image\\\\\\\" or \\\\\\\"observation/wrist_image\\\\\\\",\\\\n        # you should change it below.\\\\n        # Pi0 models support three image inputs at the moment: one third-person view,\\\\n        # and two wrist views (left and right). If your dataset does not have a particular type\\\\n        # of image, e.g. wrist images, you can comment it out here and replace it with zeros like we do for the\\\\n        # right wrist image below.\\\\n        base_image = _parse_image(data[\\\\\\\"observation/image\\\\\\\"])\\\\n        wrist_image = _parse_image(data[\\\\\\\"observation/wrist_image\\\\\\\"])\\\\n\\\\n        # Create inputs dict. Do not change the keys in the dict below.\\\\n        inputs = {\\\\n            \\\\\\\"state\\\\\\\": data[\\\\\\\"observation/state\\\\\\\"],\\\\n            \\\\\\\"image\\\\\\\": {\\\\n                \\\\\\\"base_0_rgb\\\\\\\": base_image,\\\\n                \\\\\\\"left_wrist_0_rgb\\\\\\\": wrist_image,\\\\n                # Pad any non-existent images with zero-arrays of the appropriate shape.\\\\n                \\\\\\\"right_wrist_0_rgb\\\\\\\": np.zeros_like(base_image),\\\\n            },\\\\n            \\\\\\\"image_mask\\\\\\\": {\\\\n                \\\\\\\"base_0_rgb\\\\\\\": np.True_,\\\\n                \\\\\\\"left_wrist_0_rgb\\\\\\\": np.True_,\\\\n                # We only mask padding images for pi0 model, not pi0-FAST. Do not change this for your own dataset.\\\\n                \\\\\\\"right_wrist_0_rgb\\\\\\\": np.True_ if self.model_type == _model.ModelType.PI0_FAST else np.False_,\\\\n            },\\\\n        }\\\\n\\\\n        # Pad actions to the model action dimension. Keep this for your own dataset.\\\\n        # Actions are only available during training.\\\\n        if \\\\\\\"actions\\\\\\\" in data:\\\\n            inputs[\\\\\\\"actions\\\\\\\"] = data[\\\\\\\"actions\\\\\\\"]\\\\n\\\\n        # Pass the prompt (aka language instruction) to the model.\\\\n        # Keep this for your own dataset (but modify the key if the instruction is not\\\\n        # stored in \\\\\\\"prompt\\\\\\\"; the output dict always needs to have the key \\\\\\\"prompt\\\\\\\").\\\\n        if \\\\\\\"prompt\\\\\\\" in data:\\\\n            inputs[\\\\\\\"prompt\\\\\\\"] = data[\\\\\\\"prompt\\\\\\\"]\\\\n\\\\n        return inputs\\\\n\\\\n\\\\n@dataclasses.dataclass(frozen=True)\\\\nclass LiberoOutputs(transforms.DataTransformFn):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    This class is used to convert outputs from the model back the the dataset specific format. It is\\\\n    used for inference only.\\\\n\\\\n    For your own dataset, you can copy this class and modify the action dimension based on the comments below.\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n    def __call__(self, data: dict) -> dict:\\\\n        # Only return the first N actions -- since we padded actions above to fit the model action\\\\n        # dimension, we need to now parse out the correct number of actions in the return dict.\\\\n        # For Libero, we only return the first 7 actions (since the rest is padding).\\\\n        # For your own dataset, replace `7` with the action dimension of your dataset.\\\\n        return {\\\\\\\"actions\\\\\\\": np.asarray(data[\\\\\\\"actions\\\\\\\"][:, :7])}\\\\n\\\", \\\"stderr\\\": \\\"\\\", \\\"return_code\\\": 0}, \\\"error\\\": null, \\\"metadata\\\": {\\\"elapsed_seconds\\\": 0.39}}\"}"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"6cfe60aa-a404-463e-9147-a2e703fdbada","uuid":"a524cc02-f964-4769-a471-19df962eb09d","timestamp":"2026-05-17T16:51:06.555Z","tool_use_result":{"content":"{\"result\":\"{\\\"success\\\": true, \\\"data\\\": {\\\"stdout\\\": \\\"import dataclasses\\\\n\\\\nimport einops\\\\nimport numpy as np\\\\n\\\\nfrom openpi import transforms\\\\nfrom openpi.models import model as _model\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef make_libero_example() -> dict:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Creates a random input example for the Libero policy.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    return {\\\\n        \\\\\\\"observation/state\\\\\\\": np.random.rand(8),\\\\n        \\\\\\\"observation/image\\\\\\\": np.random.randint(256, size=(224, 224, 3), dtype=np.uint8),\\\\n        \\\\\\\"observation/wrist_image\\\\\\\": np.random.randint(256, size=(224, 224, 3), dtype=np.uint8),\\\\n        \\\\\\\"prompt\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"do something\\\\\\\",\\\\n    }\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef _parse_image(image) -> np.ndarray:\\\\n    image = np.asarray(image)\\\\n    if np.issubdtype(image.dtype, np.floating):\\\\n        image = (255 * image).astype(np.uint8)\\\\n    if image.shape[0] == 3:\\\\n        image = einops.rearrange(image, \\\\\\\"c h w -> h w c\\\\\\\")\\\\n    return image\\\\n\\\\n\\\\n@dataclasses.dataclass(frozen=True)\\\\nclass LiberoInputs(transforms.DataTransformFn):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    This class is used to convert inputs to the model to the expected format. For Libero, we only return the first 7 actions (since the rest is padding).\\\\n        # For your own dataset, replace `7` with the action dimension of your dataset.\\\\n        return {\\\\\\\"actions\\\\\\\": np.asarray(data[\\\\\\\"actions\\\\\\\"][:, :7])}\\\\n\\\", \\\"stderr\\\": \\\"\\\", \\\"return_code\\\": 0}, \\\"error\\\": null, \\\"metadata\\\": {\\\"elapsed_seconds\\\": 0.39}}\"}","structuredContent":{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"import dataclasses\\n\\nimport einops\\nimport numpy as np\\n\\nfrom openpi import transforms\\nfrom openpi.models import model as _model\\n\\n\\ndef make_libero_example() -> dict:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Creates a random input example for the Libero policy.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    return {\\n        \\\"observation/state\\\": np.random.rand(8),\\n        \\\"observation/image\\\": np.random.randint(256, size=(224, 224, 3), dtype=np.uint8),\\n        \\\"observation/wrist_image\\\": np.random.randint(256, size=(224, 224, 3), dtype=np.uint8),\\n        \\\"prompt\\\": \\\"do something\\\",\\n    }\\n\\n\\ndef _parse_image(image) -> np.ndarray:\\n    image = np.asarray(image)\\n    if np.issubdtype(image.dtype, np.floating):\\n        image = (255 * image).astype(np.uint8)\\n    if image.shape[0] == 3:\\n        image = einops.rearrange(image, \\\"c h w -> h w c\\\")\\n    return image\\n\\n\\n@dataclasses.dataclass(frozen=True)\\nclass LiberoInputs(transforms.DataTransformFn):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    This class is used to convert inputs to the model to the expected format. Do not change the keys in the dict below.\\n        inputs = {\\n            \\\"state\\\": data[\\\"observation/state\\\"],\\n            \\\"image\\\": {\\n                \\\"base_0_rgb\\\": base_image,\\n                \\\"left_wrist_0_rgb\\\": wrist_image,\\n                # Pad any non-existent images with zero-arrays of the appropriate shape.\\n                \\\"right_wrist_0_rgb\\\": np.zeros_like(base_image),\\n            },\\n            \\\"image_mask\\\": {\\n                \\\"base_0_rgb\\\": np.True_,\\n                \\\"left_wrist_0_rgb\\\": np.True_,\\n                # We only mask padding images for pi0 model, not pi0-FAST. Keep this for your own dataset.\\n        # Actions are only available during training.\\n        if \\\"actions\\\" in data:\\n            inputs[\\\"actions\\\"] = data[\\\"actions\\\"]\\n\\n        # Pass the prompt (aka language instruction) to the model.\\n        # Keep this for your own dataset (but modify the key if the instruction is not\\n        # stored in \\\"prompt\\\"; the output dict always needs to have the key \\\"prompt\\\").\\n        if \\\"prompt\\\" in data:\\n            inputs[\\\"prompt\\\"] = data[\\\"prompt\\\"]\\n\\n        return inputs\\n\\n\\n@dataclasses.dataclass(frozen=True)\\nclass LiberoOutputs(transforms.DataTransformFn):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    This class is used to convert outputs from the model back the the dataset specific format. It is\\n    used for inference only.\\n\\n    For your own dataset, you can copy this class and modify the action dimension based on the comments below.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __call__(self, data: dict) -> dict:\\n        # Only return the first N actions -- since we padded actions above to fit the model action\\n        # dimension, we need to now parse out the correct number of actions in the return dict.\\n        # For Libero, we only return the first 7 actions (since the rest is padding).\\n        # For your own dataset, replace `7` with the action dimension of your dataset.\\n        return {\\\"actions\\\": np.asarray(data[\\\"actions\\\"][:, :7])}\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.39}}"}}}

Save a replay video of the episode\\n            suffix = \\\"success\\\" if done else \\\"failure\\\"\\n            task_segment = task_description.replace(\\\" \\\", \\\"_\\\")\\n            imageio.mimwrite(\\n                pathlib.Path(args.video_out_path) / f\\\"rollout_{task_segment}_{suffix}.mp4\\\",\\n                [np.asarray(x) for x in replay_images],\\n                fps=10,\\n            )\\n\\n            # Log current results\\n            logging.info(f\\\"Success: {done}\\\")\\n            logging.info(f\\\"# episodes completed so far: {total_episodes}\\\")\\n            logging.info(f\\\"# successes: {total_successes} ({total_successes / total_episodes * 100:.1f}%)\\\")\\n\\n        # Log final results\\n        logging.info(f\\\"Current task success rate: {float(task_successes) / float(task_episodes)}\\\")\\n        logging.info(f\\\"Current total success rate: {float(total_successes) / float(total_episodes)}\\\")\\n\\n    logging.info(f\\\"Total success rate: {float(total_successes) / float(total_episodes)}\\\")\\n    logging.info(f\\\"Total episodes: {total_episodes}\\\")\\n\\n\\ndef _get_libero_env(task, resolution, seed):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Initializes and returns the LIBERO environment, along with the task description.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    task_description = task.language\\n    task_bddl_file = pathlib.Path(get_libero_path(\\\"bddl_files\\\")) / task.problem_folder / task.bddl_file\\n    env_args = {\\\"bddl_file_name\\\": task_bddl_file, \\\"camera_heights\\\": resolution, \\\"camera_widths\\\": resolution}\\n    env = OffScreenRenderEnv(**env_args)\\n    env.seed(seed)  # IMPORTANT: seed seems to affect object positions even when using fixed initial state\\n    return env, task_description\\n\\n\\ndef _quat2axisangle(quat):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    Copied from robosuite: https://github.com/ARISE-Initiative/robosuite/blob/eafb81f54ffc104f905ee48a16bb15f059176ad3/robosuite/utils/transform_utils.py#L490C1-L512C55\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    # clip quaternion\\n    if quat[3] > 1.0:\\n        quat[3] = 1.0\\n    elif quat[3] < -1.0:\\n        quat[3] = -1.0\\n\\n    den = np.sqrt(1.0 - quat[3] * quat[3])\\n    if math.isclose(den, 0.0):\\n        # This is (close to) a zero degree rotation, immediately return\\n        return np.zeros(3)\\n\\n    return (quat[:3] * 2.0 * math.acos(quat[3])) / den\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)\\n    tyro.cli(eval_libero)\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.7}}"}}}

> /root/autodl-tmp/importance_margin_vla_compress/dryrun_pi05_v2.py << 'SCRIPT_EOF'\n\"\"\"Dry-run using standard LeRobot preprocessor pipeline.\"\"\"\nimport os, sys, types, math, logging\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom collections import deque\n\nos.environ[\"CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES\"] = \"0\"\nos.environ[\"MUJOCO_GL\"] = \"egl\"\nos.environ[\"HF_HOME\"] = \"/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache\"\n\nimport numpy as np\nimport torch\nimport torch._dynamo; torch._dynamo.config.disable = True\n\nlogging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, force=True, format=\"%(asctime)s %(message)s\")\nlog = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\nCKPT_DIR = \"/root/autodl-tmp/pi05-libero-finetuned\"\nDEVICE = \"cuda:0\"\nSEED = 7\nREPLAN_STEPS = 5\n\nlog.info(\"Loading Pi0.5...\")\nfrom lerobot.policies.pi05.modeling_pi05 import PI05Policy\npolicy = PI05Policy.from_pretrained(CKPT_DIR)\n\n# Fix embed_image\npaligemma_expert = policy.model.paligemma_with_expert\n\ndef patched_embed_image(self, image):\n    out_dtype = image.dtype\n    if image.dtype != torch.float32:\n        image = image.to(torch.float32)\n    image_outputs = self.paligemma.model.vision_tower(image)\n    selected_image_feature = image_outputs.last_hidden_state\n    features = self.paligemma.model.multi_modal_projector(selected_image_feature)\n    features = features * self.paligemma.config.text_config.hidden_size**0.5\n    if features.dtype != out_dtype:\n        features = features.to(out_dtype)\n    return features\n\npaligemma_expert.embed_image = types.MethodType(patched_embed_image, paligemma_expert)\nlog.info(\"Patched embed_image\")\n\npolicy = policy.to(DEVICE)\npolicy.eval()\nlog.info(f\"Model loaded: {sum(p.numel() for p in policy.parameters())/1e6:.1f}M params\")\n\n# Load preprocessor and postprocessor from checkpoint\nlog.info(\"Loading preprocessor/postprocessor...\")\nfrom lerobot.processor.pipeline import DataProcessorPipeline\npreprocessor = DataProcessorPipeline.from_pretrained(\n    CKPT_DIR, \"policy_preprocessor.json\", local_files_only=True\n)\npostprocessor = DataProcessorPipeline.from_pretrained(\n    CKPT_DIR, \"policy_postprocessor.json\", local_files_only=True\n)\nlog.info(\"Preprocessor/postprocessor loaded\")\n\n# LIBERO setup\nfrom libero.libero import benchmark, get_libero_path\nfrom libero.libero.envs import OffScreenRenderEnv\n\nnp.random.seed(SEED)\ntask_suite = benchmark.get_benchmark_dict()[\"libero_object\"]()\n\ndef quat_to_axisangle(quat):\n    q = quat.copy()\n    if q[3] > 1.0:\n        q[3] = 1.0\n    elif q[3] < -1.0:\n        q[3] = -1.0\n    den = np.sqrt(1.0 - q[3]**2)\n    if abs(den) < 1e-8:\n        return np.zeros(3, dtype=np.float32)\n    return (q[:3] * 2.0 * np.arccos(q[3]) / den).astype(np.float32)\n\ntask = task_suite.get_task(0)\ninit_states = task_suite.get_task_init_states(0)\nbddl = Path(get_libero_path(\"bddl_files\")) / task.problem_folder / task.bddl_file\nenv = OffScreenRenderEnv(bddl_file_name=str(bddl), camera_heights=256, camera_widths=256)\nenv.seed(SEED)\ntask_desc = task.language\nlog.info(f\"Task: {task_desc}\")\n\nsuccesses = 0\nfor ep in range(5):\n    env.reset()\n    obs = env.set_init_state(init_states[ep])\n    for _ in range(10):\n        obs, _, _, _ = env.step([0.0]*6 + [-1.0])\n\n    action_queue = deque()\n    done = False\n\n    for t in range(280):\n        if not action_queue:\n            # Build raw observation dict (what the preprocessor expects)\n            base_img = obs[\"agentview_image\"]\n            wrist_img = obs[\"robot0_eye_in_hand_image\"]\n            base_t = torch.from_numpy(base_img.copy()).permute(2, 0, 1).float() / 255.0\n            wrist_t = torch.from_numpy(wrist_img.copy()).permute(2, 0, 1).float() / 255.0\n\n            eef_pos = obs[\"robot0_eef_pos\"]\n            eef_quat = obs[\"robot0_eef_quat\"]\n            gripper_qpos = obs[\"robot0_gripper_qpos\"]\n            state = np.concatenate([eef_pos, quat_to_axisangle(eef_quat), gripper_qpos]).astype(np.float32)\n            state_t = torch.from_numpy(state)\n\n            raw_batch = {\n                \"observation.images.image\": base_t,\n                \"observation.images.image2\": wrist_t,\n                \"observation.state\": state_t,\n                \"task\": task_desc,\n            }\n\n            # Use standard preprocessor\n            processed = preprocessor(raw_batch)\n            # Move to device\n            batch = {}\n            for k, v in processed.items():\n                if isinstance(v, torch.Tensor):\n                    batch[k] = v.to(DEVICE)\n                else:\n                    batch[k] = v\n\n            with torch.no_grad():\n                actions = policy.predict_action_chunk(batch)\n\n            # Use standard postprocessor\n            post_input = {\"action\": actions}\n            post_output = postprocessor(post_input)\n            action_np = post_output[\"action\"][0].cpu().numpy()\n\n            if t == 0 and ep == 0:\n                log.info(f\"  Preprocessed batch keys: {list(batch.keys())}\")\n                for k, v in batch.items():\n                    if isinstance(v, torch.Tensor):\n                        log.info(f\"    {k}: shape={v.shape} dtype={v.dtype}\")\n                log.info(f\"  Raw action shape: {actions.shape}\")\n                log.info(f\"  Post-processed action shape: {action_np.shape}\")\n                log.info(f\"  First 3 actions (7d): {action_np[:3].tolist()}\")\n\n            for a in action_np[:REPLAN_STEPS]:\n                action_queue.append(a[:7])\n\n        action = action_queue.popleft()\n        obs, reward, done, info = env.step(action.tolist())\n        if done:\n            successes += 1\n            log.info(f\"  ep{ep}: SUCCESS at step {t}\")\n            break\n\n    if not done:\n        log.info(f\"  ep{ep}: FAIL (timeout)\")\n\nenv.close()\nlog.info(f\"\\nResult: {successes}/5 = {successes/5*100:.0f}%\")\nSCRIPT_EOF\necho \\\"---\\\\nlanguage:\\\\n- en\\\\nlibrary_name: lerobot\\\\npipeline_tag: robotics\\\\ntags:\\\\n- vision-language-action\\\\n- imitation-learning\\\\n- lerobot\\\\ninference: false\\\\ndatasets:\\\\n- HuggingFaceVLA/libero\\\\nlicense: gemma\\\\n---\\\\n\\\\n# π₀.₅ (Pi05 Libero) (LeRobot)\\\\n\\\\nπ₀.₅ is a Vision-Language-Action (VLA) model with open-world generalization from Physical Intelligence, co-trained on robot demonstrations and large-scale multimodal data to execute long-horizon tasks in unseen real-world environments.\\\\n\\\\nCheckpoint trained and evaluated on LIBERO tasks\\\\n\\\\n**Note:** This model currently supports only the flow-matching action head for π₀.₅ training and inference. \\\\nOther components from the original work (e.g., subtask prediction, action tokenization, or RL) were not released upstream and are not included here, though the LeRobot team is actively working to support them.\\\\n\\\\n**Original paper:** π0.5: A Vision-Language-Action Model with Open-World Generalization  \\\\n**Reference implementation:** https://github.com/Physical-Intelligence/openpi  \\\\n**LeRobot implementation:** Follows the original reference code for compatibility.\\\\n\\\\n\\\\n## Model description\\\\n\\\\n- **Inputs:** images (multi-view), proprio/state, optional language instruction\\\\n- **Outputs:** continuous actions\\\\n- **Training objective:** flow matching\\\\n- **Action representation:** continuous\\\\n- **Intended use:** Base model to fine tune on your specific use case\\\\n\\\\n\\\\n## Quick start (inference on a real batch)\\\\n\\\\n### Installation\\\\n\\\\n```bash\\\\npip install \\\\\\\"lerobot[pi]@git+https://github.com/huggingface/lerobot.git\\\\\\\"\\\\n```\\\\nFor full installation details (including optional video dependencies such as ffmpeg for torchcodec), see the official documentation: https://huggingface.co/docs/lerobot/installation\\\\n\\\\n### Load model + dataset, run `select_action`\\\\n\\\\n```python\\\\nimport torch\\\\nfrom lerobot.datasets.lerobot_dataset import LeRobotDataset\\\\nfrom lerobot.policies.factory import make_pre_post_processors\\\\n\\\\n# Swap this import per-policy\\\\nfrom lerobot.policies.pi05 import PI05Policy\\\\n\\\\n# load a policy\\\\nmodel_id = \\\\\\\"lerobot/pi05_libero_finetuned\\\\\\\"  # <- swap checkpoint\\\\ndevice = torch.device(\\\\\\\"cuda\\\\\\\" if torch.cuda.is_available() else \\\\\\\"cpu\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\npolicy = PI05Policy.from_pretrained(model_id).to(device).eval()\\\\n\\\\npreprocess, postprocess = make_pre_post_processors(\\\\n    policy.config,\\\\n    model_id,\\\\n    preprocessor_overrides={\\\\\\\"device_processor\\\\\\\": {\\\\\\\"device\\\\\\\": str(device)}},\\\\n)\\\\n# load a lerobotdataset\\\\ndataset = LeRobotDataset(\\\\\\\"lerobot/libero\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n# pick an episode\\\\nepisode_index = 0\\\\n\\\\n# each episode corresponds to a contiguous range of frame indices\\\\nfrom_idx = dataset.meta.episodes[\\\\\\\"dataset_from_index\\\\\\\"][episode_index]\\\\nto_idx   = dataset.meta.episodes[\\\\\\\"dataset_to_index\\\\\\\"][episode_index]\\\\n\\\\n# get a single frame from that episode (e.g. the first frame)\\\\nframe_index = from_idx\\\\nframe = dict(dataset[frame_index])\\\\n\\\\nbatch = preprocess(frame)\\\\nwith torch.inference_mode():\\\\n    pred_action = policy.select_action(batch)\\\\n    # use your policy postprocess, this post process the action\\\\n    # for instance unnormalize the actions, detokenize it etc..\\\\n    pred_action = postprocess(pred_action)\\\\n```\\\\n\\\\n\\\\n## Training step (loss + backward)\\\\n\\\\nIf you’re training / fine-tuning, you typically call `forward(...)` to get a loss and then:\\\\n\\\\n```python\\\\npolicy.train()\\\\nbatch = dict(dataset[0])\\\\nbatch = preprocess(batch)\\\\n\\\\nloss, outputs = policy.forward(batch)\\\\nloss.backward()\\\\n\\\\n```\\\\n\\\\n> Notes:\\\\n> \\\\n> - Some policies expose `policy(**batch)` or return a dict; keep this snippet aligned with the policy API.\\\\n> - Use your trainer script (`lerobot-train`) for full training loops.\\\\n\\\\n\\\\n## How to train / fine-tune\\\\n\\\\n```bash\\\\nlerobot-train \\\\\\\\\\\\n  --dataset.repo_id=${HF_USER}/&lt;dataset> \\\\\\\\\\\\n  --output_dir=./outputs/[RUN_NAME]  --policy.device=cuda \\\\\\\\\\\\n  --steps=100000 \\\\\\\\\\\\n  --batch_size=4\\\\n```\\\\n\\\\nAdd policy-specific flags below:\\\\n\\\\n- `-policy.chunk_size=...`\\\\n- True\n\nlogging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, force=True, format=\"%(asctime)s %(message)s\")\nlog = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\nCKPT_DIR = \"/root/autodl-tmp/pi05-libero-finetuned\"\nDEVICE = \"cuda:0\"\nSEED = 7\nREPLAN_STEPS = 5\n\nlog.info(\"Loading Pi0.5...\")\nfrom lerobot.policies.pi05.modeling_pi05 import PI05Policy\npolicy = PI05Policy.from_pretrained(CKPT_DIR)\n\n# Fix embed_image\npaligemma_expert = policy.model.paligemma_with_expert\n\ndef patched_embed_image(self, image):\n    out_dtype = image.dtype\n    if image.dtype != torch.float32:\n        image = image.to(torch.float32)\n    image_outputs = self.paligemma.model.vision_tower(image)\n    selected_image_feature = image_outputs.last_hidden_state\n    features = self.paligemma.model.multi_modal_projector(selected_image_feature)\n    features = features * self.paligemma.config.text_config.hidden_size**0.5\n    if features.dtype != out_dtype:\n        features = features.to(out_dtype)\n    return features\n\npaligemma_expert.embed_image = types.MethodType(patched_embed_image, paligemma_expert)\nlog.info(\"Patched embed_image\")\n\npolicy = policy.to(DEVICE)\npolicy.eval()\nlog.info(f\"Model loaded: {sum(p.numel() for p in policy.parameters())/1e6:.1f}M params\")\n\n# Load standard pre/post processors\nlog.info(\"Loading preprocessor/postprocessor via make_pre_post_processors...\")\nfrom lerobot.policies.factory import make_pre_post_processors\npreprocess, postprocess = make_pre_post_processors(\n    policy.config,\n    CKPT_DIR,\n    preprocessor_overrides={\"device_processor\": {\"device\": str(DEVICE)}},\n)\nlog.info(\"Preprocessor/postprocessor loaded successfully\")\n\n# LIBERO setup\nfrom libero.libero import benchmark, get_libero_path\nfrom libero.libero.envs import OffScreenRenderEnv\n\nnp.random.seed(SEED)\ntask_suite = benchmark.get_benchmark_dict()[\"libero_object\"]()\n\ndef quat_to_axisangle(quat):\n    q = quat.copy()\n    if q[3] > 1.0: q[3] = 1.0\n    elif q[3] < -1.0: q[3] = -1.0\n    den = np.sqrt(1.0 - q[3]**2)\n    if abs(den) < 1e-8:\n        return np.zeros(3, dtype=np.float32)\n    return (q[:3] * 2.0 * np.arccos(q[3]) / den).astype(np.float32)\n\n# Test on task 0 and task 1\nfor task_id in [0, 1]:\n    task = task_suite.get_task(task_id)\n    init_states = task_suite.get_task_init_states(task_id)\n    bddl = Path(get_libero_path(\"bddl_files\")) / task.problem_folder / task.bddl_file\n    env = OffScreenRenderEnv(bddl_file_name=str(bddl), camera_heights=256, camera_widths=256)\n    env.seed(SEED)\n    task_desc = task.language\n    log.info(f\"Task {task_id}: {task_desc}\")\n\n    successes = 0\n    for ep in range(3):\n        env.reset()\n        obs = env.set_init_state(init_states[ep])\n        for _ in range(10):\n            obs, _, _, _ = env.step([0.0]*6 + [-1.0])\n\n        action_queue = deque()\n        done = False\n\n        for t in range(280):\n            if not action_queue:\n                base_img = obs[\"agentview_image\"]\n                wrist_img = obs[\"robot0_eye_in_hand_image\"]\n                base_t = torch.from_numpy(base_img.copy()).permute(2, 0, 1).float() / 255.0\n                wrist_t = torch.from_numpy(wrist_img.copy()).permute(2, 0, 1).float() / 255.0\n\n                eef_pos = obs[\"robot0_eef_pos\"]\n                eef_quat = obs[\"robot0_eef_quat\"]\n                gripper_qpos = obs[\"robot0_gripper_qpos\"]\n                state = np.concatenate([eef_pos, quat_to_axisangle(eef_quat), gripper_qpos]).astype(np.float32)\n                state_t = torch.from_numpy(state)\n\n                raw_obs = {\n                    \"observation.images.image\": base_t,\n                    \"observation.images.image2\": wrist_t,\n                    \"observation.state\": state_t,\n                    \"task\": task_desc,\n                }\n\n                batch = preprocess(raw_obs)\n\n                with torch.no_grad():\n                    actions = policy.predict_action_chunk(batch)\n\n                post_out = postprocess({\"action\": actions})\n                action_np = post_out[\"action\"][0].cpu().numpy()\n\n                if t == 0 and ep == 0:\n                    log.info(f\"  Batch keys: {list(batch.keys())}\")\n                    log.info(f\"  Action shape: {action_np.shape}\")\n                    log.info(f\"  First 3 actions (full 7d):\")\n                    for i in range(min(3, len(action_np))):\n                        log.info(f\"    step{i}: {action_np[i, :7].tolist()}\")\n\n                for a in action_np[:REPLAN_STEPS]:\n                    action_queue.append(a[:7])\n\n            action = action_queue.popleft()\n            obs, reward, done, info = env.step(action.tolist())\n            if done:\n                successes += 1\n                log.info(f\"  T{task_id} ep{ep}: SUCCESS at step {t}\")\n                break\n\n        if not done:\n            log.info(f\"  T{task_id} ep{ep}: FAIL (timeout)\")\n\n    env.close()\n    log.info(f\"Task {task_id}: {successes}/3\")\n\nlog.info(\"Done\")\nSCRIPT_EOF\necho {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"This session is being continued from a previous conversation that ran out of context. The summary below covers the earlier portion of the conversation.\n\nSummary:\n1. Primary Request and Intent:\n   The user tasked me to fix a Pi0.5 eval transformers compatibility issue (`'Tensor' object has no attribute 'pooler_output'`) on server cqa1-33015, then run two eval conditions:\n   - **Baseline**: Pi0.5 on LIBERO-Object, 10 tasks × 50 episodes = 500 episodes, no pruning → `eval_results/eval_pi05_baseline.json`\n   - **Random pruning k=0.5 true_removal**: Remove 50% vision tokens randomly → `eval_results/eval_pi05_random_prune_k05.json`\n   \n   Instructions: Do a 5-episode dry-run first, stop and report if baseline SR < 20%. Use `submit_training_job` for full eval.\n\n2. Key Technical Concepts:\n   - Pi0.5 VLA model (4143.4M params) using PaliGemma vision encoder with SigLIP\n   - LeRobot PI05Policy with `predict_action_chunk` and `_preprocess_images`\n   - transformers 4.53.2 `get_image_features()` breaking change: added `/ hidden_size**0.5` division not present in openpi version\n   - Flow-matching action head, chunk_size=50, 7-dim actions, 8-dim state\n   - State discretization into 256 bins for text tokenization\n   - LIBERO-Object benchmark with OffScreenRenderEnv (robosuite)\n   - Preprocessor/postprocessor pipeline architecture in LeRobot\n   - Vision token pruning via monkey-patching `embed_prefix`\n\n3. Files and Code Sections:\n\n   - **`/root/autodl-tmp/importance_margin_vla_compress/eval_pi05_libero.py`** (FIXED)\n     - Main eval script for Pi0.5 baseline and random pruning\n     - Critical fix: `patched_embed_image` bypasses `get_image_features` to call `vision_tower` + `multi_modal_projector` directly\n     - Full script rewritten with the fix (via `cat > ... << 'SCRIPT_EOF'`)\n     - Key patch code:\n     ```python\n     def patched_embed_image(self, image):\n         out_dtype = image.dtype\n         if image.dtype != torch.float32:\n             image = image.to(torch.float32)\n         image_outputs = self.paligemma.model.vision_tower(image)\n         selected_image_feature = image_outputs.last_hidden_state\n         features = self.paligemma.model.multi_modal_projector(selected_image_feature)\n         features = features * self.paligemma.config.text_config.hidden_size**0.5\n         if features.dtype != out_dtype:\n             features = features.to(out_dtype)\n         return features\n     ```\n\n   - **`/root/autodl-tmp/importance_margin_vla_compress/dryrun_pi05.py`**\n     - 5-episode dry-run on task 0 with manual preprocessing\n     - Used to verify the vision fix works — actions became reasonable (mean=0.15, std=0.37)\n     - Result: 0/5 success but actions vary per episode (model IS seeing images)\n\n   - **`/root/autodl-tmp/importance_margin_vla_compress/dryrun_pi05_v3.py`**\n     - Dry-run using standard `make_pre_post_processors` from LeRobot\n     - Uses `HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1` and symlinked paligemma tokenizer cache\n     - Currently running (PID 328528) but appears stuck in model loading phase (~31GB RAM, no GPU usage yet)\n\n   - **`/root/miniconda3/lib/python3.12/site-packages/lerobot/policies/pi05/modeling_pi05.py`**\n     - LeRobot PI05Policy source — `predict_action_chunk` at line 1231, `_preprocess_images` at line 1142, `embed_image` at line 437\n     - Original `embed_image` expects `get_image_features().pooler_output * hidden_size**0.5`\n\n   - **`/root/miniconda3/lib/python3.12/site-packages/lerobot/policies/pi05/processor_pi05.py`**\n     - `Pi05PrepareStateTokenizerProcessorStep`: normalizes state → discretizes into 256 bins → formats as \"Task: ..., State: ...; Action: \"\n     - Standard tokenizer: `google/paligemma-3b-pt-224` (NOT cached locally)\n\n   - **`/root/miniconda3/lib/python3.12/site-packages/transformers/models/paligemma/modeling_paligemma.py`**\n     - Current `get_image_features` (line 232): adds `/ hidden_size**0.5` division — THIS IS THE BREAKING CHANGE\n\n   - **`/root/autodl-tmp/openpi-repo/src/openpi/models_pytorch/transformers_replace/models/paligemma/modeling_paligemma.py`**\n     - openpi's `get_image_features` (line 232): NO division — returns raw projected features\n\n   - **`/root/autodl-tmp/pi05-libero-finetuned/config.json`**\n     - Model config: 2 real image features + 1 empty camera, image_resolution=[224,224], chunk_size=50, n_action_steps=50, compile_model=true\n     - action dim=7, state dim=8, tokenizer_max_length=200\n\n   - **`/root/autodl-tmp/pi05-libero-finetuned/policy_preprocessor.json`**\n     - Pipeline: rename → to_batch → normalizer → pi05_prepare_state_tokenizer → tokenizer(google/paligemma-3b-pt-224) → device\n\n   - **`/root/autodl-tmp/openpi-repo/examples/libero/main.py`**\n     - Reference openpi LIBERO eval: uses replan_steps=5, 256x256 env resolution, same state format, same warmup\n\n4. Errors and Fixes:\n   - **`'Tensor' object has no attribute 'pooler_output'`** (original error):\n     - Root cause: transformers 4.53.2 `get_image_features` returns raw tensor instead of object with `.pooler_output`\n     - Previous worker's fix was insufficient: `isinstance(image_outputs, torch.Tensor)` check worked to avoid crash but the scaling was wrong\n     \n   - **Vision features 45x too small** (discovered by me):\n     - Root cause: New transformers divides by `sqrt(hidden_size)` in `get_image_features`, then the patch multiplies back by `sqrt(hidden_size)`, netting `raw_features` instead of `raw_features * sqrt(hidden_size)`\n     - Fix: Bypass `get_image_features` entirely, call `vision_tower` + `multi_modal_projector` directly, then multiply by `sqrt(hidden_size)`\n     - Verification: After fix, actions have reasonable magnitudes and vary per episode (model sees images)\n     \n   - **Still 0% SR after vision fix** (ongoing):\n     - After fixing vision scaling, model produces reasonable actions but 0/5 on task 0\n     - Attempted standard LeRobot preprocessor pipeline but `google/paligemma-3b-pt-224` tokenizer not cached locally (no internet on server)\n     - Created symlinks from gemma-2b tokenizer cache to fake paligemma cache path\n     - v3 dry-run using `make_pre_post_processors` started but appears stuck in model loading\n\n   - **SSH timeout issues**: `ssh_execute` has 30-second timeout; long-running commands need `nohup ... &` pattern\n   - **`ssh_read_only` pipe blocking**: Commands with `|` are blocked in read-only mode; needed `ssh_execute` for grep piped commands\n\n5. Problem Solving:\n   - **Solved**: Identified the critical vision feature scaling bug (45x factor). The openpi `get_image_features` returns raw projected features, but transformers 4.53.2 adds `/ sqrt(hidden_size)` division. The original `embed_image` expected raw features and multiplied by `sqrt(hidden_size)`.\n   - **Partially solved**: Vision fix applied and verified (actions now reasonable), eval script rewritten.\n   - **Ongoing**: Model still gets 0/5 on task 0 even with fixed vision. Investigating whether this is:\n     (a) A remaining preprocessing bug (tokenizer mismatch, state encoding difference)\n     (b) A genuine model quality issue for this specific checkpoint\n     (c) An issue with the eval pipeline (action format, env interaction)\n   - Currently attempting to use the standard LeRobot preprocessor pipeline to eliminate manual preprocessing as a variable. The v3 dry-run (PID 328528) is the latest attempt but may be stuck.\n\n6. All User Messages:\n   - **Message 1** (task assignment): Detailed task to fix Pi0.5 eval transformers compatibility and run baseline + random pruning evals on LIBERO-Object. Provided background on error, known clues (openpi patches), environment details, fix strategies (check patches → monkey-patch → downgrade transformers), experiment targets, and key details about Pi0.5's architecture.\n   - **Message 2**: \"Tool loaded.\" (after ToolSearch for kill_job)\n   - **Message 3**: \"CRITICAL: Respond with TEXT ONLY...\" (summary request)\n\n7. Pending Tasks:\n   - Determine why model gets 0% SR even with vision fix (is it preprocessing or model quality?)\n   - If preprocessing issue: fix it and verify with dry-run\n   - If model quality issue: report baseline SR < 20% and stop per instructions\n   - Run full baseline eval (10 tasks × 50 episodes) if dry-run shows > 20% SR\n   - Run random pruning k=0.5 eval (10 tasks × 50 episodes)\n   - Register artifacts with eval results\n   - Report: baseline SR, pruned SR, cliff-edge analysis\n\n8. Current Work:\n   I was running `dryrun_pi05_v3.py` (PID 328528) which uses the standard LeRobot `make_pre_post_processors` instead of manual preprocessing, to determine if the 0% SR is caused by a preprocessing bug vs model quality. Removing the last one gives 256 values. \n\nNow I'm tracing through how `np.digitize` actually maps values to bins. For values outside the range, it returns 0 for anything below the minimum and the total bin count for anything above the maximum, which after subtracting 1 gives us indices from -1 to 255. For values within the [-1, 1] range, each value gets assigned to the appropriate bin index. The digitize approach gives 127 for v=0 while the manual rounding approach gives 128, so there's a small discrepancy between the two methods, but it's only off by one value which shouldn't significantly impact the results.\n\nNow I'm realizing I need to check whether LeRobot actually clips states to [-1, 1] before digitization. The NormalizerProcessorStep just applies (x - mean) / std normalization without clipping, so values could theoretically exceed the [-1, 1] range depending on the training data statistics. This means my script's clipping step might be handling cases differently than the actual LeRobot pipeline does.\n\nLooking at the digitization logic more carefully, when `np.digitize` encounters values outside the [-1, 1] bin range, values below -1 return 0 which becomes -1 after subtracting 1—that's an invalid negative index. {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01XNMnXbGeST26TC3e1BJb2u","type":"tool_result","content":"{\"result\":\"{\\\"success\\\": true, \\\"data\\\": {\\\"stdout\\\": \\\"        self.eval()\\\\n\\\\n        # Action queue logic for n_action_steps > 1\\\\n        if len(self._action_queue) == 0:\\\\n            actions = self.predict_action_chunk(batch)[:, : self.config.n_action_steps]\\\\n            # Transpose to get shape (n_action_steps, batch_size, action_dim)\\\\n            self._action_queue.extend(actions.transpose(0, 1))\\\\n\\\\n        return self._action_queue.popleft()\\\\n\\\\n    @torch.no_grad()\\\\n    def predict_action_chunk(self, batch: dict[str, Tensor], **kwargs: Unpack[ActionSelectKwargs]) -> Tensor:\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Predict a chunk of actions given environment observations.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        self.eval()\\\\n\\\\n        # Prepare inputs\\\\n        images, img_masks = self._preprocess_images(batch)\\\\n        tokens, masks = batch[f\\\\\\\"{OBS_LANGUAGE_TOKENS}\\\\\\\"], batch[f\\\\\\\"{OBS_LANGUAGE_ATTENTION_MASK}\\\\\\\"]\\\\n\\\\n        # Sample actions using the model (pass through RTC kwargs, no separate state needed for PI05)\\\\n        actions = self.model.sample_actions(images, img_masks, tokens, masks, **kwargs)\\\\n\\\\n        # Unpad actions to actual action dimension\\\\n        original_action_dim = self.config.output_features[ACTION].shape[0]\\\\n        actions = actions[:, :, :original_action_dim]\\\\n\\\\n        return actions\\\\n\\\\n    def forward(self, batch: dict[str, Tensor], reduction: str = \\\\\\\"mean\\\\\\\") -> tuple[Tensor, dict]:\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Run the batch through the model and compute the loss for training.\\\\n\\\\n        Args:\\\\n            batch: Training batch containing observations and actions.\\\\n            reduction: How to reduce the loss. Options:\\\\n                - \\\\\\\"mean\\\\\\\": Return scalar mean loss (default, backward compatible)\\\\n                - \\\\\\\"none\\\\\\\": Return per-sample losses of shape (batch_size,) for RA-BC weighting\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        # Prepare inputs\\\\n        images, img_masks = self._preprocess_images(batch)\\\\n        tokens, masks = batch[f\\\\\\\"{OBS_LANGUAGE_TOKENS}\\\\\\\"], batch[f\\\\\\\"{OBS_LANGUAGE_ATTENTION_MASK}\\\\\\\"]\\\\n\\\\n        actions = self.prepare_action(batch)\\\\n\\\\n        # {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01YPMqp5V4PBBxCR3z2jM4kL","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"thinking","thinking":"Now I can see the full picture. The `_preprocess_images` function:\n\n1. Converts to float32\n2. Detects channels-first format (our images are [B, C, H, W])\n3. Permutes to [B, H, W, C]\n4. **Resizes with padding** to `self.config.image_resolution` (224×224) if needed\n5. Normalizes from [0,1] to [-1,1] (`img * 2.0 - 1.0`)\n6. Permutes back to [B, C, H, W]\n\n\n7.
